Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the diff_geom structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 408. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 108 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 408 + O(1/n^108). Summing over all modes and applying the diff_geom convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the isomorphism class fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
